Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the fundamental_group structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 99. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 109 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the fundamental_group space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 99 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 109. QED.
